Fully lifted interpolating comparisons of bilinearly indexed random processes
Mihailo Stojnic

Abstract




Abstract

A powerful statistical interpolating concept, which we call
fully lifted
(fl), is introduced and presented while establishing a connection between bilinearly indexed random processes and their corresponding fully decoupled (linearly indexed) comparative alternatives. Despite on occasion very involved technical considerations, the final interpolating forms and their underlying relations admit rather elegant expressions that provide conceivably highly desirable and useful tool for further studying various different aspects of random processes and their applications. We also discuss the generality of the considered models and show that they encompass many well known random structures and optimization problems to which then the obtained results automatically apply.

Index Terms: Random processes; comparison principles, lifting
.

1
Introduction

In this paper, we study random processes interpolations and resulting comparisons. Many excellent results on these topics have been obtained in various directions over the last several decades. Probably two of the most prominent ones are
the Slepian’s max
[
53
]
and the Gordon’s minmax
[
24
]
principle (see also, e.g.,
[
70
,
15
,
16
,
30
]
). Brief history and development reviews, with a hint at a rather massive range of applications spanning the most diverse scientific fields, can be found in, e.g.,
[
69
,
1
,
34
,
33
,
72
]
. The methods turned out to be particularly successful over the last 20 years when utilized as the main probabilistic foundation in the analysis of random structures and optimization problems (see, e.g.,
[
25
,
73
,
38
,
39
,
40
,
41
]
for the Slepian and
[
58
,
55
,
54
,
59
,
56
,
57
]
for both Slepian and Gordon principles based mechanisms). As is well known, in such structures, in large dimensional settings, one often encounters the appearance of the so-called
phase-transition
(PT) phenomenon. This phenomenon means that the underlying random structure or optimization problem exhibits a sharp change in behavior, as one moves from one region of the system parameters to another. For example, for a particular algorithm, a random optimization problem is highly likely solvable throughout a range of system parameters and then highly unlikely solvable outside such a range. Quite surprisingly, not only were the techniques from, say,
[
58
,
55
,
54
,
56
,
59
]
strong enough to provide an excellent qualitative performance characterizations of the underlying problems, they also turned out to be capable of doing so on an ultimate, PT, precision level. In other words, methods employed in
[
58
,
55
,
54
,
56
,
59
]
were strong enough to precisely pinpoint both the existence of the PT phenomena and their exact locations in the space of problem parameters.

On the other hand, in some of the scenarios such a methodology (despite providing useful performance characterizations) still needs a substantial upgrading to achieve the full PT level of precision (more on this can be found in, e.g.,
[
25
,
73
,
38
,
39
,
40
,
41
]
for quadratic (or particularly polynomial/tensorial) max type forms, and in, e.g.,
[
64
,
65
,
62
,
61
,
66
,
67
]
and references therein for various other forms including both quadratic and bilinear max and minmax types of forms). As discussed in
[
25
,
73
,
38
,
39
,
40
,
41
]
regarding the quadratic/tensorial max forms, and in
[
68
]
regarding the forms from
[
64
,
65
,
62
,
61
,
66
,
67
]
, the foundational blocks of the needed upgrades are rooted in the core improvements of the underlying random processes’ comparisons. As many of generic random structures still lack a full PT level analytical characterization, the need for establishing optimal comparisons’ upgradings is rather pressing. The machinery of
[
69
,
68
]
and the introduction of a
partial lifting
concept made a solid progress in that direction. Here though, we move things to a whole another level and create a new interpolating mechanism to which we refer as
fully lifted
(fl). As it will be clear throughout the presentation, the key idea will be in successively increasing the number of the so-called nested levels of lifting. To make the reasoning and the underlying mathematics more instructive and easier to follow and to slowly successively introduce all key technical ingredients, we, in Sections
2
and
3
, present separately the results for the first and second level of lifting and then, in Section
4
, the corresponding generalization for any
r
-th (
r\in{\mathbb{N}}
), level of lifting.

2
A bilinear comparison form – first level of lifting

For given two sets,
{\cal X}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
with
{\bf x}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}
and
{\cal Y}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
with
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m}
, vector
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}=0
, vector
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}=0
, and real parameters
\beta>0
and
s
, we consider function

f(G,u^{(4,1)},u^{(4,2)},{\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},\beta,s)=\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}}\log\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta\left(({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})\right)}\right)^{s}\right).

(1)

Behavior of this function in random mediums is of our prevalent interest. In particular, we consider
(m\times n)
dimensional matrices
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
with i.i.d. standard normal components and independent (of
G
and among themselves) random variables
u^{(4,1)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})
and
u^{(4,2)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})
. For a scalar
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1}]
, the following function turns out to be critically important for studying
f(G,u^{(4,1)},u^{(4,2)},{\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},\beta,s)

\xi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s)\triangleq{\mathbb{E}}_{G,u^{(4,2)}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}\log{\mathbb{E}}_{u^{(4,1)}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta\left(({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4)}\right)}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}}.

(2)

Throughout the paper,
{\mathbb{E}}
with a subscript denotes expectation with respect to subscript specified randomness. On the other hand,
{\mathbb{E}}
without subscript denotes the expectation with respect to any underlying randomness. To study properties of
\xi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s)
, we
find it quite convenient to follow into the footsteps of
[
69
,
68
]
and consider the following interpolating function
\psi(\cdot)

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,u^{(4,2)},{\bf u}^{(2,2)},{\bf h}^{(2)}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}\log{\mathbb{E}}_{u^{(4,1)},{\bf u}^{(2,1)},{\bf h}^{(1)}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}},

(3)

where

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

(4)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}.

In (
3
),
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
and
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
are
m
dimensional vectors of i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
and
{\bf p}_{1}
, respectively. Similarly,
{\bf h}^{(1)}
and
{\bf h}^{(2)}
are
n
dimensional vectors of i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
and
{\bf q}_{1}
, respectively. These four vectors are assumed to be independent among themselves and of
G
,
u^{(4,1)}
, and
u^{(4,2)}
as well.

It is not that difficult to see that one basically has
D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
as a
bilinearly
indexed (Gaussian) random process. One also easily observes that
\xi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s)=\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
and since
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
is typically easier to handle than
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
, one would like to connect
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
to
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
as a way of connecting
\xi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s)
to
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
. This would essentially connect the original,
bilinearly
indexed, process to two decoupled,
linearly
indexed, comparative alternatives.

For the convenience of the exposition that follows below, we set

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(1)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}.

(5)

Assuming that
G_{j,1:n}
denotes the
j
-th row of
G
and that
{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}
is the
j
-th component of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}
, one from (
2
) has

\displaystyle{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G_{j,1:n}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},1\leq j\leq m.

(6)

Also, it is rather trivial that for any fixed
i_{1}
, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}
are i.i.d. standard normals, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
and
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
are zero-mean Gaussians with respective variances
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
and
{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}
, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}
and
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}
are zero-mean Gaussians with respective variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
and
{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}
. Setting
{\mathcal{U}}_{r}=\{u^{(4,r)},{\bf u}^{(2,r)},{\bf h}^{(r)}\},r\in\{1,2\}
, one then easily rewrites (
3
) as

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}\log{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}},

(7)

where
\beta_{i_{1}}=\beta\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}
and

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

\displaystyle D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}))

\displaystyle A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle e^{\beta_{i_{1}}D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}

\displaystyle C^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}.

(8)

The readers familiar with the statistical physics replica theory related fields, may recognize the nesting type of averaging of successively added Gaussian sequences often seen in the so-called replica symmetry breaking (rsb) schemes, pioneered by Parisi in
[
43
,
42
,
44
,
45
]
. As is well known in these fields, the idea of replicated systems, in both replica symmetric (rs) and replica symmetry breaking (rsb) regimes, is to eventually utilize the
index decoupling
enabled by the averaging over large number of replicas. While replicated systems appear exactly nowhere in (
7
) and (
2
), a trace of their decoupling flair might be recognized if one notes the existence of the (bilinearly) coupled
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
and fully decoupled
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
. In particular, if an analytical relation between
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
and
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
can be established, then the coupled
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
might turn out to be, partially or even completely, characterizable via the decoupled
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
. This would, in a way, be similar to how decoupled replicated systems, rather magically, faithfully characterize the non-replicated coupled ones in various statistical physics considerations. While no rigorous mathematical justification can be put behind such a reasoning, the underlying intuition seems rather convincing and imposes, almost as an imperative, further exploring of the existence of a potential connection between
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
and
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
.

Since comparing
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,1)
and
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,0)
naturally presents itself as a logical path in such an exploration, studying monotonicity of
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)
seems as a good starting point on such a path. To that end, we start by considering its derivative

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}\log{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}

(9)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{s{\bf m}_{1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{l}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt},

where

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}=\left(\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)})}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

(10)

Utilizing (
2
) we find

\displaystyle\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d\left(\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})\right)}{dt}

(11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

Combining (
10
) and (
11
) we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

(12)

\displaystyle+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,2)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}.

The above terms can be rearranged into three clearly distinguishable groups that depend on
G
,
{\mathcal{U}}_{2}
, and
{\mathcal{U}}_{1}
, respectively

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{G}+\bar{T}_{2}+\bar{T}_{1},

(13)

where

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,2)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle\bar{T}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}u^{(4,1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}.

(14)

Combining (
9
) and (
13
) we have

\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{s{\bf m}_{1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\left(\bar{T}_{G}+\bar{T}_{2}+\bar{T}_{1}\right).

(15)

Finally, we recognize the following seven objects placed in the three mentioned groups that will play a key role in the ensuing computations.

\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}=\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\beta\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\left(T_{G}+T_{2}+T_{1}\right),

(16)

where

\displaystyle T_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}},

(17)

and

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{1,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{1,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right).

(18)

The above equations (
15
)-(
2
) are, in principle, sufficient to determine the
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)
’s derivative. We will handle each of the above seven terms separately. While doing so, we will heavily rely on
[
68
]
. Moreover, to facilitate the presentation, whenever given a chance, we will try to parallel the presentation of
[
68
]
as much as possible.

2.1
Computing
\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

As mentioned above, we will separately handle all the terms appearing in (
2
). However, we also carefully choose the order in which we do so. In particular, we first handle the three terms from the last group (
T_{1,1,j}
,
T_{1,2}
, and
T_{1,3}
), then the three terms from the middle group (
T_{2,1,j}
,
T_{2,2}
, and
T_{2,3}
), and then
T_{G,j}
as well. We call the last group
T_{1}
–group, the middle one
T_{2}
–group, and the first one
T_{G}
–group.

2.1.1
Handling
T_{1}
–group

We handle separately each of the three terms from
T_{1}
–group.

Determining

T_{1,1,j}

We start by relying on the Gaussian integration by parts to obtain

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(19)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)})\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right).

Now, we observe that the inner expectation on the most right hand side is structurally identical to the one considered in the second part of Section 3.1 in
[
68
]
. That quantity was computed based on the analysis from Section 2.1.2 of
[
68
]
. One notes that the above minimal scaling adjustment (together with a trivial replacement of
c_{3}
by
{\bf m}_{1}
) then immediately gives

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

(20)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2}\right)\right),

where from
[
68
]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)=\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2},

(21)

with

\displaystyle\Theta_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}+A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}\right)}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle\Theta_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

We find it convenient to observe the following as well

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(i_{1})})^{2}}\beta_{i_{1}}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right).

Consider the operator

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\triangleq{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\cdot\right)\right),

(24)

and set

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\gamma_{01}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{02}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\gamma_{0}(i_{1},p_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{1}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2})\right)

\displaystyle.\gamma_{2}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2}).

(25)

Clearly, all the above
\gamma
’s are valid measures. For example, one has the following

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\gamma_{01}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

(26)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1,

where the second to last equality follows by the definitions of
Z
and
C^{(i_{1})}
from (
2
). Combining (
26
) with the trivial fact
\gamma_{01}^{(1)}\geq 0
, one indeed has that
\gamma_{01}^{(1)}
is a measure. The proofs for the remaining
\gamma
’s are identical and we omit them. Throughout the paper, we denote by
\langle\cdot\rangle_{a}
the average with respect to measure
a
. Also, all the above
\gamma
measures (and
\Phi(\cdot)
operators) are clearly functions of
t
. Consequently, all functions that depend on
\gamma
’s throughout the paper will be functions of
t
. To lighten the notation we skip stating this explicitly all the time and, instead, assume it as implicitly trivial.

From (
2.1.1
)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left(\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1)\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right).

From (
2.1.1
), we also have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

(28)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

Combining (
20
), (
2.1.1
), and (
28
) we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)\right)

(29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})\beta^{2}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1})\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

Determining

T_{1,2}

As above, we follow the Gaussian integration by parts path to find

\displaystyle T_{1,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(30)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right).

The inner expectation on the most right hand side is structurally identical to the one considered in the third part of Section 3.1 in
[
68
]
. Following the analysis from Section 2.1.3 of
[
68
]
, one, after trivially replacing
c_{3}
by
{\bf m}_{1}
, immediately has

\displaystyle T_{1,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

(31)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}+A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})(1-{\bf m}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

Following the above considerations leading to (
29
), we can then also immediately write

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}

(32)

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

Determining

T_{1,3}

We again proceed via Gaussian integration by parts to obtain

\displaystyle T_{1,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(33)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}(u^{(4,1)}u^{(4,1)})\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right).

The last inner expectation is structurally identical to the one considered in the fourth part of Section 3.1 in
[
68
]
which was computed based on the analysis from Section 2.1.4 of
[
68
]
. As a result of that analysis we immediately have

\displaystyle T_{1,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

(34)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}+A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})(1-{\bf m}_{1})}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

Analogously to (
32
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

2.1.2
Handling
T_{2}
–group

Similarly to above, we handle separately each of the three terms that
T_{2}
’s contribution is comprised of.

Determining

T_{2,1,j}

Relying on the Gaussian integration by parts we write

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{{\bf p}_{1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right).

For the convenience of further exposition, we write the above as

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}=T_{2,1,j}^{c}+T_{2,1,j}^{d},

(37)

where

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{{\bf p}_{1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right).

(38)

One now observes that
T_{2,1,j}^{c}
scaled by
{\bf p}_{1}
is structurally identical to the term considered in the first part of Section
2.1.1
scaled by
({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})
. That means that we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}^{c}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}}{{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}}

(39)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

We now focus on
T_{2,1,j}^{d}
. To that end we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)=-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}.

Moreover, we find

\displaystyle\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}\right)}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}=\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{d(A^{(p_{1},p_{2})})}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2)}}

(41)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}.

A combination of (
2.1.2
) and (
41
) gives

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)=-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}.

(42)

Combining further (
2.1.2
) and (
42
), we obtain

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{{\bf p}_{1}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

(43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p,1)}}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

We find it useful to note the following as well

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}^{d}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

(44)

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p,1)}}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\beta_{p_{1}}\beta_{i_{1}}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

A combination of (
37
), (
39
), and (
44
) produces

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

(45)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

Determining

T_{2,2}

As usual, we proceed by relying on the Gaussian integration by parts to obtain

\displaystyle T_{2,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(46)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right).

Later on, it will turn out as useful to write the above as

\displaystyle T_{2,2}=T_{2,2}^{c}+T_{2,2}^{d},

(47)

where

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right).

(48)

Since

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf q}_{1}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}={\bf q}_{1}\frac{\beta^{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}=({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\frac{\beta^{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}},

(49)

one observes that
T_{2,2}^{c}
scaled by
{\bf q}_{1}
is structurally identical to the term considered in the second part of Section
2.1.1
scaled by
({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})
. Utilizing (
32
) we then have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}^{c}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf q}_{1}\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}

(50)

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\bf q}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

To determine
T_{2,2}^{d}
, we start with

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3)}}.

(51)

Moreover,

\displaystyle\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}=\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d\sum_{q_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(q_{1})})^{s}}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}=\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{d(A^{(p_{1},p_{2})})}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}

(52)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}.

Combining (
51
) and (
52
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}.

(53)

A further combination of (
2.1.2
) and (
53
) gives

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

(54)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\bf q}_{1}\frac{\beta^{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}}\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z}

\displaystyle\times\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(p_{1})})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Similarly to what was done above when considering
T_{2,1,j}^{d}
, we here note

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}^{d}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{(C^{(i_{1})})}

(55)

\displaystyle\times\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(p_{1})})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

A combination of (
47
), (
50
), and
(
55
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf q}_{1}\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}

(56)

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\bf q}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

Determining

T_{2,3}

Gaussian integration by parts also gives

\displaystyle T_{2,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(57)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}(u^{(4,2)}u^{(4,2)})\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle+{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right).

As usual, we find it useful to rewrite the above as

\displaystyle T_{2,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{2,3}^{c}+T_{2,3}^{d},

(58)

where

\displaystyle T_{2,3}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,3}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right).

(59)

It is again not that difficult to observe that
\frac{T_{2,3}^{c}}{{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}}=\frac{T_{1,3}}{{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}}
, which together with (
2.1.1
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}^{c}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}

(60)

\displaystyle\times+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

To find
T_{2,3}^{d}
, we start by writing

\displaystyle\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{du^{(4,2)}}=-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ}{du^{(4,2)}}.

We also have

\frac{dZ}{du^{(4,2)}}=\frac{d\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{du^{(4,2)}}=s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{d(A^{(p_{1},p_{2})})}{du^{(4,2)}}=s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}.

(62)

Connecting (
2.1.2
) and (
62
), we find

\displaystyle\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)=-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}.

Further connection between (
2.1.2
) and (
2.1.2
) gives

\displaystyle T_{2,3}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

(64)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{t}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{(C^{(i_{1})})}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(p_{1})})}\beta_{p_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\Bigg.\Bigg).

As earlier, we find it useful to note

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}^{d}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{(C^{(i_{1})})}

(65)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(p_{1})})}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

A combination of (
58
), (
60
), and (
65
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}

(66)

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

2.1.3
Handling
T_{G}
–group

We start by recalling on

\displaystyle T_{G,j}={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)={\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right).

(67)

Gaussian integration by parts gives

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(68)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{G}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

After setting

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\Theta_{G,1}\right)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Theta_{G,2}\right),

(69)

one has

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{c}+T_{G,j}^{d}.

(70)

One now recognizes that
\Theta_{G,1}
is structurally closely related to the one considered in Section 2.1.1 of
[
68
]
. The only structural difference is the power of
Z
. Instead of
1
we now have
1-{\bf m}_{1}
. Such a difference doesn’t change the analysis very much. In fact, these results are used to handle the structure considered in the first part of Section 3.1 of
[
68
]
, which is pretty much identical to the one that we have here. Following
[
68
]
’s Section 3.1, we then have

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{t}+(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right).

We can then conveniently write

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{c}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\Theta_{G,1}\right)}{\sqrt{t}}

(72)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}},

where the first two terms are obtained directly from (
2.1.1
).

To determine
T_{G,j}^{d}
, we start by writing

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}=-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}\frac{dZ}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

(73)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}\frac{d\sum_{q_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(q_{1})})^{s}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}=-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\frac{dC^{(p_{1})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{dA^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}.

Combining the expression for
\Theta_{G,2}
from (
2.1.3
) with (
73
), we find

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

(74)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}.

Combining
T_{G,j}^{d}
from (
2.1.3
) and (
74
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{d}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Theta_{G,2}\right)}{\sqrt{t}}

(75)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

A further combination of (
70
), (
72
), and (
75
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

(76)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

2.1.4
Connecting all pieces together

We now connect together all the pieces obtained above. To do so, we utilize (
16
) and (
2
) to write

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\beta\sqrt{n}}\left(\Omega_{G}+\Omega_{1}+\Omega_{2}+\Omega_{3}\right),

(77)

where

\displaystyle\Omega_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle\Omega_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle\Omega_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle\Omega_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}}{\sqrt{t}}+\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}}.

(78)

From (
76
) we have

\displaystyle\Omega_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

(79)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

From (
29
) and (
45
), we have

\displaystyle-\Omega_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

(80)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle+{\bf p}_{1}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{0}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{0}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

From (
32
) and (
56
), we have

\displaystyle-\Omega_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

(81)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle+{\bf q}_{1}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf q}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf q}_{0}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf q}_{0}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}.

From (
2.1.1
) and (
66
), we have

\displaystyle\Omega_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

(82)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle+{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}\right)
