\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}.

Finally, combining (
77
) with (
79
)-(
82
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\phi_{1}^{(1)}+\phi_{2}^{(1)}+\phi_{01}^{(1)}+\phi_{02}^{(1)}\right),

(83)

where

\displaystyle\phi_{1}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf p}_{0}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{0}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{2}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle({\bf p}_{1}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{1}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{01}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-{\bf p}_{0})(1-{\bf q}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(1)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{02}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(s-1)(1-{\bf p}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\left({\bf q}_{0}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right)\right\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(1)}}.

(84)

We summarize the above into the following proposition.

Proposition 1
.

Consider scalar
{\bf m}_{1}
, vector
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}=0
, and vector
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}=0
. Let
k\in\{1,2\}
and
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n},u^{(4,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1},{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times 1}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times 1}
all have i.i.d. zero-mean normal components (they are then independent of each other as well). Let the variances of the components of
G
,
u^{(4,1)}
,
u^{(4,2)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
,
{\bf h}^{(1)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(2)}
be
1
,
{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{1}
,
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
, and
{\bf q}_{1}
, respectively. Let
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}=[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(k)}]
. Assume that set
{\mathcal{X}}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf x}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},1\leq i\leq l
, and set
{\mathcal{Y}}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m},1\leq i\leq l
are given and that
\beta\geq 0
and
s
are real numbers. Consider the following function

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}}\log{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}},

(85)

where

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

(86)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}.

then

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\phi_{1}^{(1)}+\phi_{2}^{(1)}+\phi_{01}^{(1)}+\phi_{02}^{(1)}\right),

(87)

where
\phi
’s are as in (
2.1.4
).

Proof.

Follows from the above presentation.
∎

3
Second level of full lifting

In this section, we show how the results of the previous section extend to the second level of lifting (this level in some scenarios can be viewed as the first non-trivial one). All key logical ingredients needed for such an extension are already present in the derivation from Section
2
. We here formalize them and effectively trace the path for full lifting with
r\in{\mathbb{N}}
levels. To facilitate the easiness of the exposition and following, we will try to parallel the derivation from Sectoin
2
as closely as possible. However, to avoid unnecessary repetitions, we often proceed at a much faster pace than in Section
2
.

We now consider vectors
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2},{\bf p}_{3}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}\geq{\bf p}_{3}=0
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}\geq{\bf q}_{3}=0
. As earlier, we take
(m\times n)
dimensional matrices
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
with i.i.d. standard normal components and independent (of
G
and among themselves) random variables
u^{(4,1)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})
,
u^{(4,2)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})
, and
u^{(4,3)}\sim{\mathcal{N}}(0,{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})
. For a vector
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1},{\bf m}_{2}]
, we consider the following interpolating function
\psi(\cdot)

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right),

(88)

where

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

(89)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})},

and
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}=[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(2k)}]
,
k\in\{1,2,3\}
. In (
88
),
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
, and
{\bf u}^{(2,3)}
are
m
dimensional vectors of i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}
, and ,
{\bf p}_{2}
, respectively. Similarly,
{\bf h}^{(1)}
,
{\bf h}^{(2)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(3)}
are
n
dimensional vectors of i.i.d. zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}
, and
{\bf q}_{2}
, respectively. These six vectors are assumed to be independent among themselves and of
G
,
u^{(4,1)}
,
u^{(4,2)}
, and
u^{(4,3)}
as well.

For the convenience of the exposition, we, analogously to (
2
), set

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(1)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(2)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(3)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}.

(90)

and recall that

\displaystyle{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G_{j,1:n}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},1\leq j\leq m.

(91)

Clearly, for any fixed
i_{1}
, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}
are i.i.d. standard normals, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(2,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,2)}
, and
{\bf u}^{(2,3)}
are zero-mean Gaussians with respective variances
{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}
,
{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}
, and
{\bf p}_{2}
, and the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}
,
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}
, and
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)}
are zero-mean Gaussians with respective variances
{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}
,
{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2}
, and
{\bf q}_{2}
. One then rewrites (
88
) as

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right),

(92)

where
\beta_{i_{1}}=\beta\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}
and now

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)})

\displaystyle D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)}+u^{(4,3)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)}))

\displaystyle A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle e^{\beta_{i_{1}}D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}

\displaystyle C^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}.

(93)

Since we are again interested in studying monotonicity of
\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)
, we start by considering its derivative

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

(94)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{d\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{d{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d\left(\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}\right)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{l}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt},

where

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}=\left(\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)}+u^{(4,3)})}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}+{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,3)})}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

(95)

Utilizing (
3
) we find

\displaystyle\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d\left(\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)}+{\bf u}^{(2,3)})\right)}{dt}

(96)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,1)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,3)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

One can then write analogously to (
16
)

\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}=\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\beta\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\left(T_{G}+T_{2}+T_{1}\right),

(97)

where

\displaystyle T_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{3,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}},

(98)

and for
k\in\{1,2,3\}

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,k)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right).

(99)

We will handle each of the above ten terms separately heavily relying on and paralleling as closely as possible the presentation of Section
2
.

3.1
Computing
\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}
on the second level of full lifting

As in Section
2
, we carefully choose the order in which we handle each of the above terms. We group the three terms indexed by
k
into three groups,
T_{k}
,
k\in\{1,2,3\}
, and first handle
k=1
scenario, then
k=2
, and then
k=3
. After that, we handle
T_{G,j}
as well.

3.1.1
Handling
T_{k}
–groups —
k=1

We handle separately each of the three terms from
T_{1}
–group.

Determining

T_{1,1,j}

Analogously to (
19
), the Gaussian integration by parts gives

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(100)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du_{j}^{(2,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right).

Moreover, analogously to (
20
), we also have

\displaystyle T_{1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\left(\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2}\right)\right).

(101)

where
\Theta_{1}
and
\Theta_{2}
are as in (
2.1.1
). Analogously to (
2.1.1
), we conveniently observe the following as well

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L^{(2)}_{1}+L^{(2)}_{2},

(102)

where

\displaystyle L^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle L^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(i_{1})})^{2}}\beta_{i_{1}}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right).

We set the operators

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)},

(104)

and consider the following weighted/product Gibbs measures

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\gamma_{01}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{02}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\gamma_{0}(i_{1},p_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{2}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}(\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{3}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2}))

(105)

As in Section
2
, all of the above
\gamma
’s are indeed valid measures. For example, we can write the following for
\gamma_{1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\gamma_{1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

(106)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1,

where the second to last equality follows by the definitions of
Z
and
C^{(i_{1})}
from (
3
). Together with the trivial fact
\gamma_{1}^{(2)}\geq 0
, (

) ensures that
\gamma_{1}^{(2)}
is indeed a measure. The proofs that
\gamma_{2}^{(2)}
and
\gamma_{3}^{(2)}
are also valid measures trivially proceed along these lines as well and we skip them. We continue the practice established in Section
2
, and denote by

the average with respect to measure
a
. Analogously to (
2.1.1
), we from (

) obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{1}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

(107)

\displaystyle+(s-1)\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Using (
2.1.1
), we also have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{\beta_{i_{1}}\Theta_{2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}\right)=L^{(2)}_{3},

(108)

where

\displaystyle L^{(2)}_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

(109)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1})\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

Combining (

), (

), and (
108
) we obtain, analogously to (

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

(110)

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

Determining

T_{1,2}

Integration by parts gives

\displaystyle T_{1,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right).

Analogously to (
32
) we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

(112)

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

Determining

T_{1,3}

We again proceed via Gaussian integration by parts to obtain

\displaystyle T_{1,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right),

and analogously to (
2.1.1
)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

3.1.2
Handling
T_{k}
–groups —
k=2

As usual, we handle separately each of the three terms contributing to
T_{2}
group.

Determining

T_{2,1,j}

Relying on the Gaussian integration by parts we write

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(115)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{1-{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\left(\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)\right)\right).

As in (
37
), we split

One now observes that

(118)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

We now focus on
T_{2,1,j}^{d}
. To that end we have

\displaystyle\left(\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)\right)=-\frac{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}.

Utilizing (
41
), we write analogously to (

)

\displaystyle T_{2,1,j}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\left(-\frac{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,2)}}\right)\right)

(120)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\right)}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p,1)}}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

We then first observe

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}^{d}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}},

(121)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})\beta^{2}

(122)

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}.

Determining

T_{2,2}

As usual, we proceed by relying on the Gaussian integration by parts to obtain

\displaystyle T_{2,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(123)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{c}+T_{2,2}^{d},

where

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)\right).

Since

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,2)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}=({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})\frac{\beta^{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}({\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,1)}{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,1)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}=({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\frac{\beta^{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}},

one observes that

scaled by

is structurally identical to the term considered in the second part of Section

({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})
. Utilizing (

\displaystyle({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})\beta^{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

To determine

, we start with

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}^{(p_{1},3,2)}}.

\displaystyle-\frac{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{1-t}.

) and (

) gives the following analogue to (

\displaystyle T_{2,2}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{1-t}\beta^{2}({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(p_{1})})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Similarly to what was done above when considering

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle.

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,2)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)\right)

As usual, we find it useful to rewrite the above as

\displaystyle T_{2,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{2,3}^{c}+T_{2,3}^{d},

\displaystyle T_{2,3}^{c}

One again observes that

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

To find

, we first write

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{dZ}{du^{(4,2)}}.

(139)

\displaystyle\times\frac{1-\frac{-{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{t}({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{(C^{(i_{1})})}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{(C^{(p_{1})})}\beta_{p_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\Bigg.\Bigg).

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}.

3.1.3
Handling

–groups —

As usual, we handle separately each of the three terms contributing to

group.

Determining

T_{3,1,j}

Relying on the Gaussian integration by parts we write

\displaystyle T_{3,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,3)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(142)

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,3)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(159)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,3)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\right).

As usual, we find it useful to rewrite the above as

\displaystyle T_{3,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{3,3}^{c}+T_{3,3}^{d},

(160)

where

\displaystyle T_{3,3}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,3)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,3)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{3,3}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,3)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\left(\frac{d}{du^{(4,3)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\right)\right)\right).

(161)

One again observes that
\frac{T_{3,3}^{c}}{{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}}=\frac{T_{2,3}}{{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}}
, which gives

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,3}^{c}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}\beta^{2}

(162)

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}.

To find
T_{3,3}^{d}
, we first write

\displaystyle\frac{d}{du^{(4,3)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}

(163)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}}\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{du^{(4,3)}}.

We then also have

\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{du^{(4,3)}}={\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t},

(164)

and

\displaystyle T_{3,3}^{d}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,3)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}

(165)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}}\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{du^{(4,3)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,3)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}}\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}{\bf m}_{1}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}-1}s\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\sqrt{t}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\sqrt{t}{\bf m}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\beta_{p_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\Bigg.\Bigg).

As earlier, we write

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,3}^{d}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}},

and then combine it with (
160
) and (
162
) to obtain the following

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}\beta^{2}

(167)

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}.

3.1.4
Handling
T_{G}
–group

We start by recalling on

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{3},{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg).

After setting

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,2}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,3}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)})\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\Theta_{G,1}^{(2)}\right)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{d_{1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Theta_{G,2}^{(2)}\right)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{d_{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Theta_{G,3}^{(2)}\right),

(169)

Gaussian integration by parts gives

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{c}+T_{G,j}^{d_{1}}+T_{G,j}^{d_{2}}.

(170)

Following (
2.1.3
)

\displaystyle\Theta_{G,1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{t}+(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right),

and then analogously to (
72
)

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{c}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}\Theta_{G,1}^{(2)}\right)}{\sqrt{t}}

(172)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}.

To determine
T_{G,j}^{d_{1}}
, we start by writing

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}},

(173)

and

\displaystyle\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf m}_{1}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{Z}\frac{dZ}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}={\bf m}_{1}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{Z}\frac{d\sum_{q_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(q_{1})})^{s}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}={\bf m}_{1}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\frac{dC^{(p_{1})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

(174)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf m}_{1}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}.

From (
173
) and (
174
) we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{1-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2})Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2-\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\frac{1}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}.

(175)

Combining the expression for
\Theta_{G,2}^{(2)}
from (
3.1.4
) with (
175
), we find

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\Theta_{G,2}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

(176)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2})Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)^{2}}\frac{1}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\Theta_{G,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\Theta_{G,2}.

Connecting (
75
),
T_{G,j}^{d_{1}}
from (
3.1.4
), and (
176
), we also obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{d_{1}}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Theta_{G,2}^{(2)}\right)}{\sqrt{t}}

(177)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{\Theta_{G,2}^{(2)}}{\sqrt{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left(\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{\Theta_{G,2}}{\sqrt{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left(\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{{\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{1}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(1)}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}.

To determine
T_{G,j}^{d_{2}}
, we start by writing similarly to (
163
)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{1}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}}\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{dZ^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{2}}\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}}\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}-1}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}{\bf m}_{1}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{Z}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}.

Combining the expression for
\Theta_{G,3}^{(2)}
from (
3.1.4
) with (
181
), we find

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2},{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Theta_{G,3}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{2}\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\beta_{i_{1}}}

(179)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\frac{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}{{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}}\frac{1}{Z}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Combining
T_{G,j}^{d_{2}}
from (
3.1.4
), and (
179
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{d_{2}}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\Theta_{G,3}^{(2)}\right)}{\sqrt{t}}

(180)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}.

A further combination of (
170
), (
172
), (
177
), and (
180
) gives

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{c}+T_{G,j}^{d_{1}}+T_{G,j}^{d_{2}}}{\sqrt{t}}

(181)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}.

3.1.5
Connecting all pieces together

As in Section
2
, we now connect together all the pieces obtained above. To do so, we utilize (
97
) and (

) to write

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\beta\sqrt{n}}\left(\Omega_{G}+\Omega_{1}+\Omega_{2}+\Omega_{3}\right),

(182)

where

\displaystyle\Omega_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle\Omega_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{3,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle\Omega_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle\Omega_{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{3,3}}{\sqrt{t}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{2,3}}{\sqrt{t}}+\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}}.

(183)

From (
181
) we have

\displaystyle\Omega_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

(184)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

From (

), (

), and (

\displaystyle-\Omega_{1}

(185)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+{\bf p}_{2}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{2}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{0}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{0}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

From (

(186)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}
