\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf q}_{1}-{\bf q}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+{\bf q}_{2}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf q}_{2}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf q}_{0}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf q}_{0}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2})({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle+{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}\right)

\displaystyle-{\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1}({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}{\bf p}_{2}{\bf q}_{2}{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}.

Finally, combining (

) with (

)-(

), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{3}^{(2)}+\phi_{01}^{(2)}+\phi_{02}^{(2)}\right),

(188)

where

\displaystyle\phi_{1}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf p}_{0}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{0}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{2}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf p}_{1}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{1}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{3}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s{\bf m}_{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle({\bf p}_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{3}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{01}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-{\bf p}_{0})(1-{\bf q}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(2)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{02}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-{\bf p}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\left({\bf q}_{0}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right)\right\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(2)}}.

(189)

We summarize the above into the following proposition.

Proposition 2
.

Let
k=\{1,2,3\}
and
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n},u^{(4,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1},{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times 1}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times 1}
all have i.i.d. zero-mean normal components (they are then independent of each other as well). For vectors
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1},{\bf m}_{2}]
,
{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},{\bf p}_{2},{\bf p}_{3}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}\geq{\bf p}_{3}=0
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},{\bf q}_{3}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}\geq{\bf q}_{3}=0
, let the variances of the components of
G
,
u^{(4,k)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,k)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}
be
1
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}{\bf q}_{k}
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}
,
{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf q}_{k}
, respectively.
Assume that set
{\mathcal{X}}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf x}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},1\leq i\leq l
, and set
{\mathcal{Y}}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m},1\leq i\leq l
are given and that
\beta\geq 0
and
s
are real numbers. Set
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}=[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(2k)}]

and consider the following function

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{3}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{2}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right),

(190)

where

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}({\bf u}^{(2,1)}+{\bf u}^{(2,2)}+{\bf u}^{(2,3)})

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(u^{(4,1)}+u^{(4,2)}+u^{(4,3)})+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf h}^{(1)}+{\bf h}^{(2)}+{\bf h}^{(3)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}.

Then

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\phi_{1}^{(2)}+\phi_{2}^{(2)}+\phi_{3}^{(2)}+\phi_{01}^{(2)}+\phi_{02}^{(2)}\right),

(192)

where
\phi
’s are as in (
3.1.5
) and
\gamma
’s are as defined in (
3.1.1
) and (
3.1.1
).

Proof.

Follows from the above presentation.
∎

4
r
-th level of full lifting

We now provide generalization to an arbitrary level,
r\in{\mathbb{N}}
, of full lifting. Section
2
provided all key principles that were used in Section
3
to present all formal mathematical steps needed to proceed to second and any higher level of lifting. We here finally formalize the final results of applying the procedure from Section
3
to any level of lifting
r\in{\mathbb{N}}
.

We now consider vectors
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{1},{\bf m}_{2},...,{\bf m}_{r}]
,

{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},...,{\bf p}_{r},{\bf p}_{r+1}]
with
{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}\geq\dots\geq{\bf p}_{r}\geq{\bf p}_{r+1}=0
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},\dots,{\bf q}_{r},{\bf q}_{r+1}]
with
{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}\geq\dots\geq{\bf q}_{r}\geq{\bf q}_{r+1}=0
. As earlier, we take
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
and, for any
k\in\{1,2,\dots,r+1\}
,
u^{(4,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{1},{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times 1}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n\times 1}
to all have i.i.d. zero-mean normal components (they are then independent of each other as well). Also, we assume that the variances of the components of
G
,
u^{(4,k)}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,k)}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}
are
1
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}{\bf q}_{k}
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}
,
{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf q}_{k}
, respectively, and
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\triangleq[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(2k)}]
. Assuming that set
{\mathcal{X}}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf x}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},1\leq i\leq l
, and set
{\mathcal{Y}}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m},1\leq i\leq l
are given and that
\beta\geq 0
and
s
are real numbers, we are interested in the following function

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}}\right),

(193)

where

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\right)

(194)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)}\right)+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf h}^{(k)}\right)^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}

As in previous sections, for the convenience of the exposition, we, analogously to (
2
), set

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}}

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{({\bf h}^{(k)})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},

(195)

and recall that

\displaystyle{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{G_{j,1:n}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}},1\leq j\leq m.

(196)

Clearly, for any fixed
i_{1}
, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}
are i.i.d. standard normals, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(2,k)}
are zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf p}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}
, the elements of
{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}
are zero-mean Gaussians with variances
{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf q}_{k}
. One then rewrites (
193
) as

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}}\right),

(197)

where
\beta_{i_{1}}=\beta\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}
and now

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\right)

\displaystyle D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)}\right)+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}\right)

\displaystyle A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle e^{\beta_{i_{1}}D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}

\displaystyle C^{(i_{1})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\right)^{s}=\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}.

(198)

We set
{\bf m}_{0}=1
and

\displaystyle\zeta_{r}\triangleq{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{{\bf m}_{0}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}},r\geq 1.

(199)

Then

\displaystyle\zeta_{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\left(\zeta_{k-1}\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}},k\geq 2,\quad\mbox{and}\quad\zeta_{1}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{\frac{{\bf m}_{1}}{{\bf m}_{0}}}\right),

(200)

and, for the completeness, we also set
\zeta_{0}=Z
. We can then write

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}}\log\left(\zeta_{r}\right)\right)

(201)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}\zeta_{r}}\frac{d\zeta_{r}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r-1}\zeta_{r}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\zeta_{r-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}-1}\frac{d\zeta_{r-1}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r-2}\zeta_{r}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\zeta_{r-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r-1}}\zeta_{r-2}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r-1}}{{\bf m}_{r-2}}-1}\frac{d\zeta_{r-2}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\zeta_{r}}\prod_{k=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}\frac{d\zeta_{1}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{1}\zeta_{r}}\prod_{k=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}\frac{d{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}\zeta_{r}}\prod_{k=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{i=1}^{l}(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt},

where the product runs in an
index decreasing order
and

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}=\left(\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}+\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)})}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)})}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

(202)

Utilizing (
4
) we find

\displaystyle\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d\left(\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},1)}+\sqrt{1-t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(2,k)})\right)}{dt}

(203)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\left(\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{2\sqrt{t}}-\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k)}}{2\sqrt{1-t}}\right).

One can then write analogously to (
16
)

\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}=\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)}{2\beta\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\left(T_{G}+\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}T_{k}\right),

(204)

where

\displaystyle T_{G}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle T_{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{j=1}^{m}\frac{T_{k,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}-\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k,3}}{\sqrt{t}},k\in\{1,2,\dots,r+1\}.

(205)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf u}^{(i_{1},3,k)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle T_{k,3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}u^{(4,k)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right).

(206)

We will first handle the 3
r+1
-tuplets,
\left(T_{k,1,j}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
,
\left(T_{k,2}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, and
\left(T_{k,3}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
and then
T_{G,j}
. As the discussion in Section
3
showed, for all our purposes, the internal connections among the components within each of these three sequences are identical. We will below consider one of them, say
\left(T_{k,1,j}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
and show how the results, obtained in Section
3
, for
r=2
, immediately extend to any
r
. To avoid unnecessarily presenting the same strategy three times, for the remaining two
r+1
-tuplets,
\left(T_{k,2}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, and
\left(T_{k,3}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, we will then quickly deduce the final results.

4.1
Scaled canceling out — successive scaling + reweightedly averaged new terms

We take a component of the sequence
\left(T_{k,1,j}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, say
T_{k_{1},1,j}
,
k_{1}\geq 2
, and show how it relates to the very next one,
T_{k_{1}+1,1,j}
. As we will formalize it below, there are two key parts of such a recursive/inductive relation: 1)
successive scaling
; and 2) appearance of an appropriately
reweightedly averaged (over a
\gamma
measure) new term
.

We start by noting that

\displaystyle T_{k_{1},1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(207)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}}}\left({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}\right)

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}-1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}-1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg),

and also

\displaystyle T_{k_{1}+1,1,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}+1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(208)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}({\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)})

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(1)}+\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)},

where

\displaystyle\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}

(209)

\displaystyle\times\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

It is useful to note here that
\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(1)}
relates to the first part mentioned above –
successive scaling
, and that
\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)}
relates to the second part – introducing a
reweightedly averaged (over a
\gamma
measure) new term
.

For
\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(1)}
we further have

\displaystyle\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}

\displaystyle\times\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}}}\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}-1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}-1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

One now observes from (
4
) that
{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}
appears only in
B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
, and then via
B^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
in
D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
, via
D^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
in
A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
, via
A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
in
C^{(i_{1},i_{2})}
, via
C^{(i_{1})}
in
Z
, and via
Z
in
\zeta_{k}
,
k\in\{1,2,\dots,r\}
. This implies that for a function
f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})

\displaystyle f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})=\left(\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\prod_{v=k_{1}-1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right),

(211)

we have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})\right)=\frac{d}{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\left(f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})\right)\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}=\sqrt{1-t}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}.

(212)

Since there is nothing specific about
k_{1}
one also has

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}\left(f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})\right)=\frac{d}{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\left(f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})\right)\frac{dB^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}=\sqrt{1-t}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})},

(213)

and

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1})}}\left(f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})\right)=\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(f_{c}(B^{(i_{1},i_{2})})\right).

(214)

A combination of (
207
), (
4.1
), and (
214
) then gives the
successive scaling
relation (which is at the heart of the
canceling out
mechanism that will be visible a bit later)

\displaystyle\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(1)}=\frac{({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})}{({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}})}T_{k_{1},1,j}.

(215)

For
\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)}
, we have

\displaystyle\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}

(216)

\displaystyle\times\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg),

where based on the sequence of equalities of (
208
) we find

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}\right)\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-2}\frac{d\zeta_{k_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}

(217)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-2}\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}\frac{d{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}\right)\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-2}\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\frac{dD^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}.

Since

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}=\frac{dB^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}=\sqrt{1-t}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})},

(218)

we, from (
217
), also have

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(2,k_{1}+1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}\right)\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-2}\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}

(219)

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}.

A combination of (
216
) and (
219
) gives

\displaystyle\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}

(220)

\displaystyle\times\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}s\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}-{\bf m}_{1}\right)\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}-2}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\sqrt{1-t}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k_{1}+1}}\zeta_{k_{1}}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\frac{\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}}}{\zeta_{v}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}}}{\zeta_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\sqrt{1-t}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle s({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\frac{\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}}}{\zeta_{v}}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\frac{\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}}}{\zeta_{v}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}}}{\zeta_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\sqrt{1-t}\beta_{p_{1}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg).

After setting

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}}}{\zeta_{k}}

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\gamma_{01}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{k=r}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{02}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{k=r}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\gamma_{0}(i_{1},p_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{k=r}^{k_{1}+1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\left(\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2})\right),

(221)

and recalling that
\zeta_{0}=Z
and
{\bf m}_{0}=1
, we, from (
220
), obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{v=r}^{k_{1}+1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}

(222)

\displaystyle\times\prod_{v=k_{1}}^{2}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}

\displaystyle\times\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\beta_{i_{1}}\beta_{p_{1}}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}}.

Combining (
208
), (
215
), and (
222
), we find

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{k_{1}+1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(1)}}{\sqrt{1-t}}+\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{\Pi_{k_{1}+1,1,j}^{(2)}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

(223)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{k_{1},1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Keeping in mind the results for first and second level of full lifting, one can then analogously easily write for the other two sequences
\left(T_{k,2}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
and
\left(T_{k,3}\right)_{k=1:r+1}

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k_{1}+1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf q}_{k_{1}}-{\bf q}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf q}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf q}_{k_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k_{1},2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\beta^{2}({\bf q}_{k_{1}}-{\bf q}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k_{1}+1,3}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf p}_{k_{1}}{\bf q}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}{\bf q}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}{\bf q}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}}{\bf q}_{k_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k_{1},3}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

(225)

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}}{\bf q}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}{\bf q}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

One now observes that adding the elements in each of these three sequences results in canceling out parts of the first and second summands. A bit later on, we will make this observation fully formal.

4.2
Handling
T_{G,j}
for
r
levels of full lifting

Introducing
{\bf m}_{r+1}=0
, we recall from (
4
)

\displaystyle T_{G,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left(\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

(226)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G}\zeta_{r}^{-1}\prod_{v=r}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G}\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}{\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}{\mathbb{E}}_{G}\left({\bf u}_{j}^{(i_{1},1)}{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}\right)\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{k_{1}=2}^{r+1}\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\prod_{v=r+1,v\neq k_{1}}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{c}+\sum_{k_{1}=2}^{r+1}T_{G,j}^{d_{k_{1}-1}},

where

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)

(227)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left(\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\Theta_{G,1}^{(2)}\right),

and

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{d_{k_{1}-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\prod_{v=r+1,v\neq k_{1}}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

(228)

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg).

Combining (
3.1.4
) and (
227
), we obtain

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{c}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\left(\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\Theta_{G,1}^{(2)}\right)

(229)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{v=r+1}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}

\displaystyle\times\Bigg(\Bigg.\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{t}+(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1})\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{2-{\bf m}_{1}}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{v=r}^{2}\frac{\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}}}{\zeta_{v}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{t}+(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1})\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{2}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{v=r}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}\frac{\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}}}{\zeta_{v}}

\displaystyle\times\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{t}+(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{2}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{v=r}^{2}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{v}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\frac{{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z}\left((C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}\beta_{i_{1}}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\sqrt{t}+(s-1)(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-2}\beta_{i_{1}}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}A^{(i_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle-(1-{\bf m}_{1})\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{({\bf x}^{(i_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}}{\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{2}}s(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\sqrt{t}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}+(s-1)\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}^{c}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\right)

(230)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}}.

To determine
T_{G,j}^{d_{k_{1}-1}}
we write

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{d_{k_{1}-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\prod_{v=r+1,v\neq k_{1}}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

(231)

\displaystyle\times\frac{d}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\left(\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-1}\right)\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\prod_{v=r+1,v\neq k_{1}}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times s\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}\right)\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-2}\prod_{k=k_{1}-1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\frac{dD^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Since

\displaystyle\frac{dD^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}=\frac{dB^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{d{\bf u}_{j}^{(p_{1},1)}}=\sqrt{t}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})},

(232)

we, from (
217
), also have

\displaystyle T_{G,j}^{d_{k_{1}-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1},{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}},\dots,{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\prod_{v=r+1,v\neq k_{1}}^{2}\zeta_{v-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{v}}{{\bf m}_{v-1}}-1}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s-1}A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}

\displaystyle\times s\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}\right)\zeta_{k_{1}-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k_{1}}}{{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}}-2}\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{1}{Z^{1-{\bf m}_{1}}}(C^{(p_{1})})^{s-1}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{p_{1}}A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}\sqrt{t}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{k=r}^{k_{1}+1}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}}}{\zeta_{k}}\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}}}{\zeta_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\times s\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}\right)\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}}}{\zeta_{k}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\frac{Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}}{\zeta_{1}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\sqrt{t}\beta_{p_{1}}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.\prod_{k=r}^{k_{1}+1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\times s\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}\right)\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\sum_{p_{1}=1}^{l}\frac{(C^{(p_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\sum_{p_{2}=1}^{l}\frac{A^{(p_{1},p_{2})}}{C^{(p_{1})}}\sqrt{t}\beta_{p_{1}}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(p_{2})}{\bf y}_{j}^{(i_{2})}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Combining (
226
), (
230
), and (
4.2
) we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{(T_{G,j}^{c}+\sum_{k_{1}=2}^{r+1}T_{G,j}^{d_{k_{1}-1}})}{\sqrt{t}}

(234)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\right)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}\sum_{k_{1}=2}^{r+1}({\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}}^{(r)}}.

With
{\bf m}_{0}=1
, we can also write

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{G,j}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\right)

(235)

\displaystyle-s\beta^{2}\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}({\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}}^{(r)}}.

4.3
Connecting everything together

We now have all the ingredients to formulate the following theorem.

Theorem 1
.

Let
r\in{\mathbb{N}}
and
k\in\{1,2,\dots,r+1\}
. For vectors
{\bf m}=[{\bf m}_{0},{\bf m}_{1},{\bf m}_{2},...,{\bf m}_{r},{\bf m}_{r+1}]
with
{\bf m}_{0}=1
and
{\bf m}_{r+1}=0
,

{\bf p}=[{\bf p}_{0},{\bf p}_{1},...,{\bf p}_{r},{\bf p}_{r+1}]
with
1\geq{\bf p}_{0}\geq{\bf p}_{1}\geq{\bf p}_{2}\geq\dots\geq{\bf p}_{r}\geq{\bf p}_{r+1}=0
and
{\bf q}=[{\bf q}_{0},{\bf q}_{1},{\bf q}_{2},\dots,{\bf q}_{r},{\bf q}_{r+1}]
with
1\geq{\bf q}_{0}\geq{\bf q}_{1}\geq{\bf q}_{2}\geq\dots\geq{\bf q}_{r}\geq{\bf q}_{r+1}=0
, let the variances of the zero-mean independent normal components of
G\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m\times n}
,
u^{(4,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}
,
{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m}
, and
{\bf h}^{(k)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n}
be
1
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}{\bf q}_{k}
,
{\bf p}_{k-1}-{\bf p}_{k}
,
{\bf q}_{k-1}-{\bf q}_{k}
, respectively. Also, let
{\mathcal{U}}_{k}\triangleq[u^{(4,k)},{\bf u}^{(2,k)},{\bf h}^{(2k)}]
. Assuming that set
{\mathcal{X}}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf x}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},1\leq i\leq l
, set
{\mathcal{Y}}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
, where
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m},1\leq i\leq l
, and scalars
\beta\geq 0
and
s\in{\mathbb{R}}
are given, and consider the following function

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}}\right),

(236)

where

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\right)

(237)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)}\right)+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf h}^{(k)}\right)^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}

Let
\zeta_{0}=Z
and let
\zeta_{k},k\geq 1
, be as defined in (
199
) and (
200
). Moreover, consider the operators

\displaystyle\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\frac{\zeta_{k-1}^{\frac{{\bf m}_{k}}{{\bf m}_{k-1}}}}{\zeta_{k}},

(238)

and set

\displaystyle\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{(C^{(i_{1})})^{s}}{Z}\frac{A^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}{C^{(i_{1})}}

\displaystyle\gamma_{01}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{k=r}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{02}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{k=r}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}(\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\gamma_{0}(i_{1},p_{2}))

\displaystyle\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\prod_{k=r}^{k_{1}+1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\left(\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\gamma_{0}(i_{1},i_{2})\times\prod_{k=k_{1}}^{1}\Phi_{{\mathcal{U}}_{k}}\gamma_{0}(p_{1},p_{2})\right).

(239)

Let also

\displaystyle\phi_{k_{1}}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-s({\bf m}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}})

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}-({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})})({\bf q}_{k_{1}-1}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})})\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{01}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1-{\bf p}_{0})(1-{\bf q}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle\phi_{02}^{(r)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(s-1)(1-{\bf p}_{0}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\left({\bf q}_{0}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}-({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right)\right\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}.

(240)

Then

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\left(\left(\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}\phi_{k_{1}}^{(r)}\right)+\phi_{01}^{(r)}+\phi_{02}^{(r)}\right).

(241)

It particular, choosing
{\bf p}_{0}={\bf q}_{0}=1
, one also has

\displaystyle\frac{d\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)}{dt}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mbox{sign}(s)\beta}{2\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}\phi_{k_{1}}^{(r)}.

(242)

Proof.

Follows through a combination of (
204
)-(
4
), (
223
)-(
225
), (
235
), after summing and canceling scaled terms and noting that, analogously to (
29
) and (
110
) for the first and second level, we have for the
r
-th level

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

(243)

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}},

and analogously to (
32
) and (
112
) for the first and second level, we have for the
r
-th level

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}},

and analogously to (
2.1.1
) and (
3.1.1
) for the first and second level, we have for the
r
-th level

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{1,3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}{\bf q}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{`})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}}.

The summing and canceling out works out in an identical way for all three sequences,
\left(T_{k,1,j}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
,
\left(T_{k,2}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
, and
\left(T_{k,3}\right)_{k=1:r+1}
. To avoid unnecessary repetition of the same mechanism, we show how it works only for the first one. Following (
223
), we first write

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{2,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}}{{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

(246)

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})\left({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\frac{{\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2}}{{\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1}}\Bigg(\Bigg.({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{1})s\beta^{2}(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})\left({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{1}-{\bf p}_{2})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s(1-{\bf m}_{1}){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{1}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\left({\bf m}_{1}-{\bf m}_{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{2}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

In general, assuming that

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{k_{1},1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{k_{1}}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg),

we, from (
223
), have

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{k_{1}+1,1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{k_{1},1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{{\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1}}{{\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}}}\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{k_{1}}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{v}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-\Bigg(\Bigg.s\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\left({\bf m}_{k_{1}}-{\bf m}_{k_{1}+1}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}+1}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{k_{1}}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}+1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{k_{1}+1}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{v}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg).

Since (
4.3
) holds for
k_{1}=1
(and, as shown in (
246
), even for
k_{1}=2
), one then, based on (
4.3
), has that it holds for any
k_{1}\in\{1,2,\dots,r+1\}
. Finally one has for the sum over
k_{1}

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\sum_{j=1}^{m}\beta_{i_{1}}\frac{T_{k_{1},1,j}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{k_{1}}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{k_{1}}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{r+1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-s\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}})

\displaystyle\times\sum_{v=1}^{k_{1}}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{v}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{r+1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{r+1}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{v}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle\times\sum_{k_{1}=v}^{r+1}({\bf p}_{k_{1}-1}-{\bf p}_{k_{1}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{0}-{\bf p}_{r+1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{r+1}{\bf p}_{v-1}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(p_{2})})^{T}{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{v}^{(r)}}.

One then analogously has for the other two sequences

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k_{1},2}}{\sqrt{1-t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf q}_{0}-{\bf q}_{r+1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{r+1}{\bf q}_{v-1}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}({\bf x}^{(p_{1})})^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{v}^{(r)}},

and

\displaystyle\sum_{k_{1}=1}^{r+1}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}\beta_{i_{1}}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\frac{T_{k_{1},3}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\beta^{2}({\bf p}_{0}{\bf q}_{0}-{\bf p}_{r+1}{\bf q}_{r+1})\Bigg(\Bigg.{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}^{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{01}^{(r)}}

\displaystyle+(s-1){\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\langle\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}^{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\rangle_{\gamma_{02}^{(r)}}\Bigg.\Bigg)

\displaystyle-s\sum_{v=1}^{r+1}{\bf p}_{v-1}{\bf q}_{v-1}\left({\bf m}_{v-1}-{\bf m}_{v}\right)

\displaystyle\times{\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\left\langle\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(p_{2})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf x}^{(p_{1})}\|_{2}\right\rangle_{\gamma_{v}^{(r)}},

A combination of (
204
)-(
4
), (
235
), and (
4.3
)-(
4.3
) finally gives (
1
) and (
241
).
∎

5
A few practical examples

As mentioned earlier, considered models and underlying biliearly indexed random processes encompass many well known random structures and optimization problems. Here, we briefly mention only a few and leave a more thorough discussion about them and many others for companion separate papers.

We first recall on the following key object of interest from Theorem
1

\psi({\cal X},{\cal Y},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,s,t)={\mathbb{E}}_{G,{\mathcal{U}}_{r+1}}\frac{1}{\beta|s|\sqrt{n}{\bf m}_{r}}\log\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{r}}\left(\dots\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{2}}\left(\left({\mathbb{E}}_{{\mathcal{U}}_{1}}\left(Z^{{\bf m}_{1}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{2}}{{\bf m}_{1}}}\right)\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{3}}{{\bf m}_{2}}}\dots\right)^{\frac{{\bf m}_{r}}{{\bf m}_{r-1}}}\right),

(252)

where

\displaystyle Z

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{l}\left(\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{l}e^{\beta D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}}\right)^{s}

\displaystyle D_{0}^{(i_{1},i_{2})}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sqrt{t}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}G{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}({\bf y}^{(i_{2})})^{T}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf u}^{(2,k)}\right)

(253)

\displaystyle+\sqrt{t}\|{\bf x}^{(i_{1})}\|_{2}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}u^{(4,k)}\right)+\sqrt{1-t}\|{\bf y}^{(i_{2})}\|_{2}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{r+1}{\bf h}^{(k)}\right)^{T}{\bf x}^{(i_{1})},

and

{\mathcal{X}}=\{{\bf x}^{(1)},{\bf x}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf x}^{(l)}\}
with
{\bf x}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{n},1\leq i\leq l
and
{\mathcal{Y}}=\{{\bf y}^{(1)},{\bf y}^{(2)},\dots,{\bf y}^{(l)}\}
with
{\bf y}^{(i)}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{m},1\leq i\leq l
. For the concreteness, we assume the so-called linear (proportional growth) regime,
\frac{m}{n}=\alpha
, where
\alpha
remains constant as
n\rightarrow\infty
.

5.1
Hopfield models

The above function
\psi(\cdot)
for
s=1
,
{\mathcal{X}}=\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n}
, and
{\mathcal{Y}}={\mathbb{S}}^{m}
(where
{\mathbb{S}}^{m}
is
m
-dimensional unit sphere) i.e.,
\psi(\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n},{\mathbb{S}}^{m},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,1,1)
, is the so called free energy of the positive square root Hopfield model. Clearly,
\psi(\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n},{\mathbb{S}}^{m},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,1,0)
is then its intended decoupled comparative counterpart. Of particular interest in random optimizations is often the so-called ground state regime, where
\beta\rightarrow\infty
. It is then rather clear that

\lim_{n,\beta\rightarrow\infty}\psi\left(\left\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}^{n},{\mathbb{S}}^{m},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,1,1\right)=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\max_{{\bf x}\in\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n}}\|G{\bf x}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}

(254)

and
\lim_{n,\beta\rightarrow\infty}\psi(\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n},{\mathbb{S}}^{m},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,1,0)
are precisely the ground state energy of the positive square root Hopfield model and its decoupled counterpart in the thermodynamic limit. More on the foundations, importance, relevance, and current state of the art results related to key aspects of the Hopfield models can be found in, e.g.,
[
28
,
46
,
27
,
47
,
50
,
5
,
7
,
71
,
65
,
9
,
74
]
.

Similarly for
s=-1
one has analogously

\lim_{n,\beta\rightarrow\infty}\psi\left(\left\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}^{n},{\mathbb{S}}^{m},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,-1,1\right)=-\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\min_{{\bf x}\in\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n}}\|G{\bf x}\|_{2}}{\sqrt{n}}

(255)

and
\lim_{n,\beta\rightarrow\infty}\psi(\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n},{\mathbb{S}}^{m},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,-1,0)
as the ground state energy of the negative square root Hopfield model and its decoupled counterpart in the thermodynamic limit.

5.2
Asymmetric Little models

The above function
\psi(\cdot)
for
s=1
,
{\mathcal{X}}=\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n}
, and
{\mathcal{X}}={\mathcal{Y}}=\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\}^{m}
cam ne viewed as a variant of the so called free energy of the positive asymmetric Little model. Clearly,
\psi(\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n},\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\}^{m},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,1,0)
is then its intended decoupled comparative counterpart. In random optimizations one is, again, often interested in

\lim_{n,\beta\rightarrow\infty}\psi\left(\left\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right\}^{n},\left\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\right\}^{n},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,1,1\right)=\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\frac{\max_{{\bf x}\in\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n}}\|G{\bf x}\|_{1}}{\sqrt{nm}}

(256)

and
\lim_{n,\beta\rightarrow\infty}\psi(\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\}^{n},\{-\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{m}}\}^{n},{\bf p},{\bf q},{\bf m},\beta,1,0)
